Background: Elderly population has been progressively rising in the world, thus the demand for anti-
page=1 block=496729294 (x=166 y=474) width=356.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
aging heath products to assure longevity as well as to ameliorate age-related complications is also on
page=1 block=496729294 (x=166 y=484) width=356.000000 height=4.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
the rise. Among various anti-aging health products, nicotinamide mononucleotide (NMN) has been gain-
page=1 block=496729294 (x=166 y=493) width=356.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
ing attentions of the consumers and the scienti?c community.
page=1 block=496729294 (x=166 y=503) width=214.000000 height=4.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
Aim of review: This article intends to provide an overview on the current knowledge on promises and
page=1 block=496729294 (x=167 y=512) width=355.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
safety concerns of NMN as an anti-aging health product.
page=1 block=496729294 (x=166 y=522) width=194.000000 height=4.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
Key scienti?c concepts ofreview: Nicotinamide adeninedinucleotide (NAD+)levelsin thebody deplete with
page=1 block=496729294 (x=163 y=530) width=359.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
aging and it is associated with downregulation of energy production in mitochondria, oxidative stress,
page=1 block=496729294 (x=166 y=541) width=356.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
DNA damage, cognitive impairment and in?ammatory conditions. However, NMN, as the precursor of
page=1 block=496729294 (x=166 y=551) width=356.000000 height=4.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
NAD+, can slow down this process by elevating NAD+levels in the body. A number of in vivo studies have
page=1 block=496729294 (x=166 y=559) width=356.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
indicated af?rmative results of therapeutic effects for various age-induced complications with NMN sup-
page=1 block=496729294 (x=166 y=570) width=356.000000 height=4.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
plementation. One preclinical and one clinical study have been conducted to investigate the safety con-
page=1 block=496729294 (x=166 y=579) width=356.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
cerns of NMN administration while a few more human clinical trials are being conducted. As there is a

Introduction
page=2 block=1577592551 (x=1 y=91) width=50.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFIBD+AdvGulliv-B wordint.min=1 wordint.max=17
The successful control of communicable diseases in the 20th
page=2 block=854587510 (x=12 y=112) width=240.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
century led to a sharp rise in the mean life expectancy of many
page=2 block=854587510 (x=1 y=122) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
countries. In 2019, number of persons, aged 65 or over was 702.9
page=2 block=854587510 (x=1 y=133) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
million in the world and it is projected to be 1548.9 million by
page=2 block=854587510 (x=1 y=143) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
2050 [1]. Percentage of global population aged 65 years or over
page=2 block=854587510 (x=1 y=154) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
in 2019 and future projections according to the medium-variant
page=2 block=854587510 (x=1 y=164) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
projection is illustrated in Fig. 1.Along with increasing elderly pop-
page=2 block=854587510 (x=1 y=175) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
ulation, the prevalence of age-related diseases such as atheroscle-
page=2 block=854587510 (x=1 y=185) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
rosis, hypertension, osteoarthritis, neurodegenerative diseases
page=2 block=854587510 (x=1 y=196) width=251.000000 height=4.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=8
including Alzheimer’s and Parkinson’s diseases, diabetes mellitus
page=2 block=854587510 (x=1 y=206) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
and cancers has gone up leading to heavy global socioeconomic
page=2 block=854587510 (x=1 y=216) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
and medical burden [2].
page=2 block=854587510 (x=1 y=227) width=91.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
Therefore, age management medical practices have been mush-

rooming in the world for recommending nutritional supplements,
page=2 block=854587510 (x=1 y=248) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
various drugs, exercise programs, hormone therapies and other
page=2 block=854587510 (x=1 y=258) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
treatments to mitigate the effect of aging. Consequently, the con-

sumer demand and the global market value for anti-aging health
page=2 block=854587510 (x=1 y=279) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
products are on the rise [3]. Excessive demand of consumers and
page=2 block=854587510 (x=1 y=290) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
high pro?t margin for manufacturers are the major driving force
page=2 block=854587510 (x=1 y=300) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
behind the release of anti-aging health products without adequate
page=2 block=854587510 (x=1 y=311) width=251.000000 height=4.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
safety testing [4]. Thus, careful comprehensive and stepwise scien-
page=2 block=854587510 (x=1 y=321) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
ti?c preclinical and clinical investigations are crucial to be

conducted.
page=2 block=854587510 (x=1 y=342) width=41.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=17
Among various anti-aging health products, nicotinamide
page=2 block=854587510 (x=12 y=352) width=240.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=10
mononucleotide (NMN) has been gaining an increasing attention
page=2 block=854587510 (x=1 y=363) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
as a promising anti-aging product. The mitochondrial decay, which
page=2 block=854587510 (x=1 y=373) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
is responsible for aging, can be reversed by the increased levels of
page=2 block=854587510 (x=1 y=384) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
nicotinamide adenine dinucleotide (NAD+) in the body. NMN is a
page=2 block=854587510 (x=1 y=392) width=251.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
precursor of NAD+ that acts as an intermediate in NAD+ biosynthe-
page=2 block=854587510 (x=1 y=403) width=251.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
sis, while dietary supplements of NMN are found to increase the
page=2 block=854587510 (x=1 y=415) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
NAD+ levels in the body [5]. NMN is a bioactive nucleotide formed
page=2 block=854587510 (x=1 y=424) width=251.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
by the reaction between a nucleoside comprising nicotinamide and
page=2 block=854587510 (x=1 y=436) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
ribose and a phosphate group [6]. It naturally presents in a variety
page=2 block=854587510 (x=1 y=447) width=251.000000 height=4.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
of plant and animal food sources. Furthermore, several studies
page=2 block=854587510 (x=1 y=457) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
have been carried out to investigate the potential of biotechnolog-
page=2 block=854587510 (x=1 y=467) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
ical production and purifying NMN from bacterial and yeasts [7].
page=2 block=854587510 (x=1 y=478) width=248.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
Other than anti-aging potential of NMN, a wide range of phar-
page=2 block=854587510 (x=12 y=488) width=240.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
macological activities have been identi?ed in a number of in vivo
page=2 block=854587510 (x=1 y=499) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
studies. The link between NMN and the incidence of Alzheimer’s
page=2 block=854587510 (x=1 y=509) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
disease, obesity and associated complications, cerebral and cardiac
page=2 block=854587510 (x=1 y=520) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
ischemia, and age- and diet-induced type 2 diabetes has been
page=2 block=854587510 (x=1 y=530) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
studied extensively [8]. Though, previous attention of scienti?c
page=2 block=854587510 (x=1 y=541) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
community has been paid on NMN only as an intermediate in

25

What is nicotinamide mononucleotide (NMN)?
page=2 block=1164440413 (x=270 y=272) width=187.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFIBD+AdvGulliv-B wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
Nicotinamide mononucleotide (NMN) exists as a and b anome-
page=2 block=1610525991 (x=281 y=293) width=240.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
ric forms while it is identi?ed as nicotinamide ribotide,
page=2 block=1610525991 (x=270 y=304) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=9
nicotinamide-1-ium-1-b-D-ribofuranoside 50-phosphate, b-
page=2 block=1610525991 (x=270 y=312) width=251.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=18
nicotinamide ribose monophosphate and 3-carbamoyl-1-[5-O-
page=2 block=1610525991 (x=270 y=325) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=8
(hydroxyphosphinato)-b-D-ribofuranosyl] pyridinium, among
page=2 block=1610525991 (x=270 y=335) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=13
others [9]. The b form is the active anomer and NMN is naturally
page=2 block=1610525991 (x=270 y=346) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
structured as a result of a reaction, which catalysed by nicoti-
page=2 block=1610525991 (x=270 y=356) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
namide phosphoribosyltransferase enzyme, between a phosphate
page=2 block=1610525991 (x=270 y=367) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
group and a nucleoside comprising nicotinamide (an amide form
page=2 block=1610525991 (x=270 y=377) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
of vitamin B3) and ribose [10]. NMN is a bioactive nucleotide with
page=2 block=1610525991 (x=270 y=388) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
a pyridinebase and its molecular weight is 334.22 g/mol. It is fairly
page=2 block=1610525991 (x=270 y=398) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
acidic and water-soluble compound. The solubility has been
page=2 block=1610525991 (x=270 y=408) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=8
reported to be 1.8 mg/mL [11] (Fig. 2).
page=2 block=1610525991 (x=270 y=419) width=147.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
NMN is mainly located in the nucleus, mitochondria and cyto-
page=2 block=1610525991 (x=281 y=429) width=240.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
plasm, whereas in the human body, it can be found in placenta tis-
page=2 block=1610525991 (x=270 y=440) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
sue and body ?uids such as blood and urine [9]. It is naturally
page=2 block=1610525991 (x=270 y=450) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
found in a variety of fruits and vegetables including immature soy-
page=2 block=1610525991 (x=270 y=461) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
bean pods, cabbage, cucumber, broccoli, tomato, mushroom and
page=2 block=1610525991 (x=270 y=471) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
Nicotinamide base
page=2 block=1666607455 (x=383 y=514) width=72.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=CPFILC+TimesNewRomanPSMT wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
Ribose sugar
page=2 block=1327006586 (x=430 y=619) width=50.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=CPFILC+TimesNewRomanPSMT wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
Phosphate group

NMN is an intermediate of NAD+ biosynthesis. NAD+ is a very

Mechanism underlying the anti-aging activity of NMN
page=3 block=112797691 (x=1 y=502) width=216.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFIBD+AdvGulliv-B wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
Aging, as a natural process is identi?ed by downregulation of
page=3 block=1045997582 (x=12 y=523) width=240.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
energy production in mitochondria of various organs such as brain,
page=3 block=1045997582 (x=1 y=533) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
adipose tissue, skin, liver, skeletal muscle and pancreas due to the
page=3 block=1045997582 (x=1 y=544) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
depletion of NAD+ [16]. NAD+ levels in the body decrease as a con-
page=3 block=1045997582 (x=1 y=553) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
sequence of increasingNAD+ consuming enzymes when aging [17].
page=3 block=1045997582 (x=1 y=563) width=251.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
These NAD+ consuming enzymes include NADase (CD38/CD157),
page=3 block=1045997582 (x=1 y=573) width=251.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
poly (ADP-ribose) polymerase (PARP), NAD+ dependent acetylase
page=3 block=1045997582 (x=1 y=584) width=251.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
(sirtuins), BST and tankyrase (TNKS) [18]. Sirtuins consume NAD+
page=3 block=1045997582 (x=1 y=594) width=251.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
in order to execute a variety of functions such as deacetylation,
page=3 block=1045997582 (x=1 y=607) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
deglutarylase, lipoamidase, demalonylase and desuccinylase activ-
page=3 block=1045997582 (x=1 y=617) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
ities. Regulation of longevity, aging and age-associated physiologi-
page=3 block=1045997582 (x=1 y=628) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
cal changes is one of the substantial aspects of sirtuin biology [19],
page=3 block=1045997582 (x=1 y=638) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
while CD38 utilises NAD+ to produce cyclic ADP-ribose and nicoti-
page=3 block=1045997582 (x=1 y=647) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
namide. Apart from that, PARP expends NAD+ to form branched
page=3 block=1045997582 (x=1 y=657) width=251.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
ADP-ribose polymers which assists in DNA repairing [20]. This
page=3 block=1045997582 (x=1 y=669) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
depleted NAD+ level by NAD+ consuming enzymes can be compen-
page=3 block=1045997582 (x=1 y=678) width=251.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
sated by administration of NMN to the body since NMN is an inter-
page=3 block=1045997582 (x=1 y=690) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
mediate compound of the NAD+ biosynthesis.
page=3 block=1045997582 (x=1 y=699) width=173.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
There are three different biosynthesis pathways to produce
page=3 block=1337335626 (x=281 y=21) width=240.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=7
NAD+ in mammalian cells including de novo synthesis from trypto-
page=3 block=1337335626 (x=270 y=29) width=251.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
phan, salvage and Preiss-Handler pathways. Among these three
page=3 block=1337335626 (x=270 y=42) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
pathways, NMN is an intermediate by-product in salvage pathway,
page=3 block=1337335626 (x=270 y=52) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
and it is involved in NAD+ biosynthesis through salvage and Preiss-
page=3 block=1337335626 (x=270 y=61) width=251.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
Handler pathways as illustrated in Fig. 3 [15]. The salvage pathway
page=3 block=1337335626 (x=270 y=73) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
is the most ef?cient and the main route for the NAD+ biosynthesis,
page=3 block=1337335626 (x=270 y=82) width=251.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
in which nicotinamide and 5-phosphoribosyl-1-pyrophosphate are
page=3 block=1337335626 (x=270 y=94) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
converted to NMN with the enzyme action of NAMPT followed by
page=3 block=1337335626 (x=270 y=105) width=251.000000 height=4.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
conjugation to ATP and conversion to NAD by NMNAT [21]. Fur-
page=3 block=1337335626 (x=270 y=115) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
thermore, NAD+ consuming enzymes are responsible for degrada-
page=3 block=1337335626 (x=270 y=124) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
tion of NAD+ and consequent formation of nicotinamide as a by-
page=3 block=1337335626 (x=270 y=134) width=251.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
product [13]. In the Preiss-Handler pathway, initially, nicotinic acid
page=3 block=1337335626 (x=270 y=146) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
is converted to nicotinic acid mononucleotide (NAMN) by nicotinic
page=3 block=1337335626 (x=270 y=157) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
acid phosphoribosyl-transferase enzyme (NAPRT) activity followed

by biosynthesis of nicotinic acid adenine dinucleotide (NAAD+)
page=3 block=1337335626 (x=270 y=176) width=251.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
from NAMN using nicotinamide/nicotinic acid mononucleotide

adenylyltransferase (NMNAT 1/2/3). Subsequently, NAAD+ is trans-
page=3 block=1337335626 (x=270 y=197) width=251.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
formed to NAD+ by NAD+ synthetase (NADS) using ATP and ammo-

nia [22].

Safety concerns and challenges
page=7 block=1258084361 (x=270 y=554) width=124.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFIBD+AdvGulliv-B wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
The ultimate goal of geroscience is to discover approaches to
page=7 block=391914049 (x=281 y=575) width=240.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
boost natural defence mechanisms and prolong healthy lifespan
page=7 block=391914049 (x=270 y=586) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
through better management of the risks posed to cells and tissues
page=7 block=391914049 (x=270 y=596) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
of humans. Due to the continuous enhancement of the living stan-
page=7 block=391914049 (x=270 y=607) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
dards of people, the desire of healthy longevity has become pro-
page=7 block=391914049 (x=270 y=617) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
gressively stronger. Conversely, there is a possibility to achieve
page=7 block=391914049 (x=270 y=628) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
the maximum life span speci?ed by the nature through decelerat-
page=7 block=391914049 (x=270 y=638) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
ing the rate of aging. Using tiny molecules to slow down the aging
page=7 block=391914049 (x=270 y=649) width=251.000000 height=4.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
process and enhancing aging-associated outcomes is a ?ourishing
page=7 block=391914049 (x=270 y=659) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
research area at the present [55]. Furthermore, at the present,
page=7 block=391914049 (x=270 y=669) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
the number of anti-aging medicines and health products are com-
page=7 block=391914049 (x=270 y=680) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
mercially available and popular among elderly consumers [56].

evidence to back up its utility is currently scarce. Despite this,
page=8 block=30578394 (x=1 y=523) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
NMN supplements are already available in the market and consid-
page=8 block=30578394 (x=1 y=533) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
erably used by consumers as anti-aging health products. Therefore,

in additionto animal models, human trails to investigate the safety

and ef?cacy of NMN should be conducted focusing on toxicological
page=8 block=30578394 (x=1 y=565) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
parameters and safe metabolite levels in the human body [8].
page=8 block=30578394 (x=1 y=575) width=235.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
Increased attention on potential pro-longevity effects of NAD+
page=8 block=30578394 (x=12 y=584) width=240.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
precursors over the recent years has led to increased consumption
page=8 block=30578394 (x=1 y=596) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
of nicotinamide either as a clinical treatment or a dietary supple-
page=8 block=30578394 (x=1 y=607) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
ment, raising concerns on the safety of their long-term usage.
page=8 block=30578394 (x=1 y=617) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
Nicotinamide has been found to cause adverse effects on several
page=8 block=30578394 (x=1 y=628) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
organs in the human body such as kidney, liver, pancreatic b-
page=8 block=30578394 (x=1 y=638) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6

page=8 block=30578394 (x=1 y=649) width=251.000000 height=4.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
and headaches [63,64]. In addition, high dose of nicotinamide sup-
page=8 block=30578394 (x=1 y=659) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
plementation is associated with decreased insulin sensitivity and
page=8 block=30578394 (x=1 y=669) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
increased oxidative stress [64]. However, NMN supplementation
page=8 block=30578394 (x=1 y=680) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
has been foundto have signi?cant recovering effectson hepatocyte
page=8 block=30578394 (x=1 y=690) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
functions and liver pathologies in early-stage of ethanol toxicity,
page=8 block=30578394 (x=1 y=701) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
instead of causing adverse effects to the liver [65]. It has also been
page=8 block=2088371948 (x=270 y=21) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
found to improve insulin sensitivity and oxidative stress in animal
page=8 block=2088371948 (x=270 y=31) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
models [23,29].
page=8 block=2088371948 (x=270 y=42) width=58.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
Although, nicotinamide supplementation induced DNA damage

renal and hepatic tissues [66], NMN has been proven to reduce

DNA damage and accumulation of ROS [32,42]. Supplementation

page=8 block=2088371948 (x=270 y=105) width=251.000000 height=4.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
[67]. Supplementation of NMN has been demonstrated to stimu-
page=8 block=2088371948 (x=270 y=115) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5

behavioural dysfunctions [35,36]. Rolfe [68]reported that adminis-
page=8 block=2088371948 (x=270 y=136) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4

page=8 block=2088371948 (x=270 y=157) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4

page=8 block=2088371948 (x=270 y=167) width=135.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5

page=9 block=1819063424 (x=1 y=324) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
did not increase nicotinamide in blood to the level which causes
page=9 block=1819063424 (x=1 y=335) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
adverse effects associated with high dose of nicotinamide. Since
page=9 block=1819063424 (x=1 y=345) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
the safety of single oral administration of NMN has only been con-
page=9 block=1819063424 (x=1 y=356) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
sidered in this study, further clinical investigations are essential to
page=9 block=1819063424 (x=1 y=366) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
be performed to assess the safety and ef?cacy of long-term admin-
page=9 block=1819063424 (x=1 y=377) width=251.000000 height=4.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
istration of NMN. The organs and the tissue NMN levels have not
page=9 block=1819063424 (x=1 y=387) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
been analysed in this study, which should be considered in future
page=9 block=1819063424 (x=1 y=397) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
clinical studies.
page=9 block=1819063424 (x=1 y=408) width=58.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
As stated by Hong et al. [15], three more human clinical studies
page=9 block=1819063424 (x=12 y=418) width=240.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4

administration. One study is a phase II study (UMIN000030609),

Japan to evaluate the safety of long-term administration of NMN,

pharmacokinetics and metabolites of NMN, and its effect on glu-

the Hiroshima University, Institute of Biomedical and Health

Sciences. The third clinical study (UMIN000036321) has been
page=9 block=1819063424 (x=1 y=523) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=7
designed to assess the consequences of oral administration of

NMN on the body composition in elderly people at the University

of Tokyo Hospital. These clinical trials are still ongoing and there
page=9 block=1819063424 (x=1 y=554) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
is no result published yet. Yoshino et al. [23] have also highlighted
page=9 block=1819063424 (x=1 y=565) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
the importance of more comprehensively assessing potential
page=9 block=1819063424 (x=1 y=575) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=8
adverse effects of NMN by conducting preclinical and clinical stud-
page=9 block=1819063424 (x=1 y=586) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
ies, considering different dietary conditions as well.
page=9 block=1819063424 (x=1 y=596) width=196.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
According to Di Stefano et al. [78], inhibitors of NMN synthesis-
page=9 block=1819063424 (x=12 y=607) width=240.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
ing enzyme (NAMPT), provided robust functional and morpholog-
page=9 block=1819063424 (x=1 y=617) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
ical protection of injured synapses and axons, regardless of

reducing NAD. But, exogenous NMN eliminated this protection
page=9 block=1819063424 (x=1 y=638) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
with the accumulation of NMN after nerve injury and NMNAT2
page=9 block=1819063424 (x=1 y=649) width=251.000000 height=4.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
degradation advanced axon degeneration. They suggested that
page=9 block=1819063424 (x=1 y=659) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=7
the relationship between the increase of NMN and axon degenera-
page=9 block=1819063424 (x=1 y=669) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
tion could be a long-hypothesised toxic and deleterious factor.
page=9 block=1819063424 (x=1 y=680) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
Poljsak and Milisav [79] reported that both NR and NMN, as vita-
page=9 block=1819063424 (x=1 y=690) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
min B3 forms and NAD+ precursors were not detected to be
page=9 block=1819063424 (x=1 y=699) width=251.000000 height=8.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=7
cancerogenic. Radenkovic and Verdin [80] also have stated that
page=9 block=1011279482 (x=270 y=21) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
since no study has reported rigorous side effects and they have

been in use for many years, therapeutic interventions including

NMN, NR, vitamin B3 and niacin supplementation, which increase
page=9 block=1011279482 (x=270 y=52) width=251.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
NAD+ levels, are likely to be fairly safe for the human use.
page=9 block=1011279482 (x=270 y=61) width=221.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
Radenkovic and Verdin [80] further explained that side effects
page=9 block=1011279482 (x=281 y=73) width=240.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
of long-term NAD upregulation are complicated to identify and
page=9 block=1011279482 (x=270 y=84) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
quantify, but they still can exist. In addition, it was illustrated that
page=9 block=1011279482 (x=270 y=94) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
prolonged high dose of vitamin B3 compounds, including NMN
page=9 block=1011279482 (x=270 y=105) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
may possibly have long-term side effects. According to Rolfe [68],
page=9 block=1011279482 (x=270 y=115) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
NAD upregulation has the possibility to make worse the
page=9 block=1011279482 (x=270 y=126) width=251.000000 height=4.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=9
senescence-associated secretory phenotype (SASP) generated by
page=9 block=1011279482 (x=270 y=136) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
senescent cells in aged tissues. A suggested mechanism to cause
page=9 block=1011279482 (x=270 y=146) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
this side effect was the suppression of 50 adenosine
page=9 block=1011279482 (x=270 y=154) width=251.000000 height=8.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=11
monophosphate-activated protein kinase and tumor protein p53,
page=9 block=1011279482 (x=270 y=167) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
directing to stimulate nuclear factor kappa B protein transcription
page=9 block=1011279482 (x=270 y=178) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
factor through p38 mitogen-activated protein kinases and raised
page=9 block=1011279482 (x=270 y=188) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
expression of in?ammatory cytokines. The cellular senescence bur-
page=9 block=1011279482 (x=270 y=199) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
den enlarged with aging, while the produced ASAP was a responsi-
page=9 block=1011279482 (x=270 y=209) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
ble factor for different age-related pathologies [81].
page=9 block=1011279482 (x=270 y=220) width=196.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
However, evidences to support these potential side effects of
page=9 block=1011279482 (x=281 y=230) width=240.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
long-term NMN usage and NAD upregulation are lacking. In fact,
page=9 block=1011279482 (x=270 y=241) width=251.000000 height=4.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
the usage of anti-aging interventions is expected to be initiated
page=9 block=1011279482 (x=270 y=251) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
at comparatively younger and healthier age than very old age.
page=9 block=1011279482 (x=270 y=262) width=251.000000 height=4.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
Therefore, identi?cation and quanti?cation of adverse effects and
page=9 block=1011279482 (x=270 y=272) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
challenges of long-term utilisation of anti-aging health products
page=9 block=1011279482 (x=270 y=282) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
is extremely essential and critical since these anti-aging products
page=9 block=1011279482 (x=270 y=293) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
are apparently used for a long time. According to Yu et al. [82],
page=9 block=1011279482 (x=270 y=303) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
NMN treatment (400 mg/kg) obstructed the exercise-induced ben-
page=9 block=1011279482 (x=270 y=314) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
e?ts of a mouse model of diet-induced obesity such as reduced
page=9 block=1011279482 (x=270 y=324) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
hepatic triglyceride accumulation, glucose stimulated insulin
page=9 block=1011279482 (x=270 y=335) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=9
secretion from islets and glucose tolerance. This ?nding should
page=9 block=1011279482 (x=270 y=345) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
be further investigated thoroughly since the exercise is one of
page=9 block=1011279482 (x=270 y=356) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
the key components for maintaining health and wellbeing of the
page=9 block=1011279482 (x=270 y=366) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
elderly.
page=9 block=1011279482 (x=270 y=377) width=28.000000 height=4.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=17
Conclusions
page=9 block=208866101 (x=270 y=408) width=47.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFIBD+AdvGulliv-B wordint.min=1 wordint.max=17
NMN is a precursor of NAD+ and an intermediate of NAD+
page=9 block=543846639 (x=281 y=427) width=240.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=7
biosynthesis, which is achieved through three pathways. NMN is
page=9 block=543846639 (x=270 y=439) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
an intermediate by-product in two of them. NAD+ levels in the
page=9 block=543846639 (x=270 y=448) width=251.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
body are depleted with aging as a result of activities of NAD+ con-
page=9 block=543846639 (x=270 y=458) width=251.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
suming enzymes. Depletion of NAD+ level is associated with down-
page=9 block=543846639 (x=270 y=469) width=251.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
regulation of energy production in mitochondria, increasing
page=9 block=543846639 (x=270 y=481) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=9
oxidative stress, DNA damage, cognitive impairments and in?am-
page=9 block=543846639 (x=270 y=492) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
matory diseases. NMN, as the precursor of NAD+, has been seen
page=9 block=543846639 (x=270 y=500) width=251.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
to likely reverse these age-related complications and slow down
page=9 block=543846639 (x=270 y=513) width=251.000000 height=4.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
the rate of aging by enhancing NAD+ levels in the body.
page=9 block=543846639 (x=270 y=521) width=212.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
Many studies have been done to explore NMN’s anti-aging
page=9 block=543846639 (x=281 y=533) width=240.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
effects in various cells and tissues. Most of the works have been
page=9 block=543846639 (x=270 y=544) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
done in vitro or in animal models. However, published reports
page=9 block=543846639 (x=270 y=554) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
about NMN’s long-term safety and clinical ef?cacy of anti-aging
page=9 block=543846639 (x=270 y=565) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
effects in humans are scarce. From the above review, it can be seen
page=9 block=543846639 (x=270 y=575) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
that only very few pre-clinical and clinical studies have been con-
page=9 block=543846639 (x=270 y=586) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
ducted to investigate the safety of long-term administration of
page=9 block=543846639 (x=270 y=596) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
NMN. A few more human clinical trials are being conducted to
page=9 block=543846639 (x=270 y=607) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
evaluatethe safety concernsof NMNsupplementation and the out-
page=9 block=543846639 (x=270 y=617) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
comes are yet to be available.
page=9 block=543846639 (x=270 y=628) width=113.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
However, many NMN anti-aging health products are already
page=9 block=543846639 (x=281 y=638) width=240.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
available in the market and manufacturers are aggressively mar-
page=9 block=543846639 (x=270 y=649) width=251.000000 height=4.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=5
keting the products using in vitro and in vivo results from the liter-
page=9 block=543846639 (x=270 y=659) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
ature. Therefore, the ?rst priority should be to establish toxicology,
page=9 block=543846639 (x=270 y=669) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
pharmacology and safety pro?les of NMN in humans, including
page=9 block=543846639 (x=270 y=680) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=6
healthy and diseased. For NMN’s anti-aging ef?cacy, the most fea-
page=9 block=543846639 (x=270 y=690) width=251.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=CPFGKE+AdvGulliv-R wordint.min=1 wordint.max=4
sible route to obtain data will probably through long-term follow-
